# 09 — Decisions Log

Format: `YYYY-MM-DD — [topic] Decision — Why`. Topics: `[core] [perm] [data] [ui] [words] [sheet] [items] [expedition] [fight] [xp] [hirelings] [packs] [adventure] [testing] [process] [ip]` (tags added 2026-10-28; search by tag to find the why). Newest first. Add a line whenever a ⚑ is ruled on.

## Standing rulings

The rulings still in force, in one place (superseded ones left out; the full history is in the archive). Each ends with the date it was made or last changed.

**Working and safety**
- [process] A choice a build agent made where the rulings were silent, already built and harmless, counts as ruled unless Francis objects at the gate — 2026-10-31
- [process] Working title Grimoire (renameable until M10); pack format id "tabletop-pack" is frozen — 2026-10-17
- [process] Every change on a branch with a pull request; merge only when check, database and layout are green and Francis says yes; GitHub deploys to Vercel (previews on the test project, production only after every check passes) — 2026-10-20, 2026-10-21
- [testing] Automated tests run against a separate test Supabase project and use invented content only; packs are private and never committed — 2026-10-19, 2026-10-21
- [process] A backup before every migration to the main project; nightly backups with a practice restore arrive in Pre-test hardening — 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22
- [process] Scope checks replace a scope freeze (format in 08); a fresh-agent code health check after every milestone; Claude Code checks for drift from parallel work — 2026-10-22

**Foundations**
- [core] Screens never write to the database: every change is a command that checks `can()` — 2026-10-19
- [core] Events are written by database triggers in the same transaction as the change; the database names entries from a fixed list of commands (GM-only names only for the GM) and sets their level; each event has an audience (everyone, the GM, or the owner and the GM) — 2026-10-20, 2026-10-21
- [core] Technical (M5 step 4, 2026-10-29): stale saves are refused by copy version (inventory lines first; code GR409, "That's changed since. Here's the latest."); each request carries the command's request id plus its number in the command, so a repeat changes nothing; who sees each log line is one function per table (a table without one is GM-only); "<name> is now <tier>" is worked out and written once by the database, never by a device — 2026-10-29
- [core] Undo: your own change, or any as GM, only if nothing changed since; one action is its request id plus its author; making and deleting are logged but not undoable, except a drop, a gift outside the party or a declined one-off (its log keeps a copy) — 2026-10-20, 2026-10-21, 2026-10-25
- [perm] Fiat is automatic when the GM uses a player's own power on that player's character; optional reason, a seal in the log, only the GM undoes it — 2026-10-21
- [data] Notes live in their own table, are never deleted, and take the stricter of their entry's and their character's visibility — 2026-10-21
- [perm] Hidden data lives in separate tables with their own access rules (monster HP in actor_secrets); hidden *fields* are retired, not secret — 2026-10-19, 2026-10-20
- [perm] **Named exception to hidden purses:** a Down or dead character's pack and purse open to the other players (Take from <name>, every take logged), enforced by the database by the character's state and closing again when Down clears (HP above the threshold, or a Helped wake-up timer ending; taken items stay taken); a hireling's pack is always open to their employer — 2026-10-29
- [core] **Deletion exceptions** (the closed list; a new kind of deletion needs a design ruling): (1) a character that was never approved can be deleted by its owner or the GM; (2) an archived character can be permanently deleted by its owner or the GM after a clear confirm, and the deletion event keeps a full copy; (3) items leaving play (dropped, used up or burnt out, given to someone outside the party, or a one-off the GM declines) are deleted, and the log keeps a copy for undo; (4) undoing a partial give merges what's left of the split-off part back into its stack, deleting the part (the log keeps it); (5) undoing an action that created an item (an activity's yield) removes that item while it's untouched, the log keeps a copy; once any of it is used, Undo is refused with a toast — 2026-10-19, 2026-10-25, 2026-10-26, 2026-11-03
- [perm] A retired campaign is read only for everyone, enforced by the database; the GM can bring it back — 2026-10-22

**Campaigns, characters, companies**
- [perm] Sign-in with a 6-digit emailed code, stay signed in, open sign-ups; mail from mail.withywindlegrove.com through Resend — 2026-10-18, 2026-10-19
- [perm] Anyone signed in can start a campaign and becomes its GM; a campaign has its own name and sigil, edited by the GM in one "Edit the campaign" panel — 2026-10-19
- [perm] Invites are a copied link or code: one use, 14 days, always as a player, with a preview before joining — 2026-10-19
- [sheet] New characters, including replacements after a death: start at level 1, the party's lowest, a fixed level, or a level the player proposes and the GM approves; the GM approves, sends back, or asks for a different level — 2026-10-22, 2026-10-25
- [sheet] A player's first approved character becomes active; several characters per player, one active; switch freely in downtime, GM approval mid-adventure (confirmed 2026-10-22) — 2026-10-18, 2026-10-19
- [data] Zero to many companies per campaign, a character in at most one; the GM assigns in R1 (votes in M8); a company with anyone in it can't be deleted — 2026-10-19, 2026-10-20
- [ui] Layout follows the window's width (phone under 600px, tablet portrait to 1023px, landscape above); Folio look, light and dark; tabs Player · Events · Pack — 2026-10-17, 2026-10-20

**Setting rules**
- [packs] The library is account-level and private; imports and updates are saved in one step, updates after a preview of what's added, changed and removed — 2026-10-22
- [perm] Players read the player-facing kinds (classes, races, items, spells, spell lists, conditions, light types, starting kits, optional rules); everything else is the GM's, enforced by the database; from M4b items only if for sale or known to the party (held by the party, in the Party stash, in a starting kit, or sent or shown by the GM); the GM's per-entry exceptions are GM-only — 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22, 2026-10-25, 2026-10-29
- [packs] Stacking: one order per campaign, homebrew on top; per-entry exceptions in the setting-rules browser; the campaign's own homebrew pack is made on first save and stays with its campaign (it can be copied to the Rules library as an independent pack) — 2026-10-22, 2026-10-25
- [packs] The in-app editor covers every kind and saves into the campaign's homebrew; editing another pack's entry makes a homebrew version that wins; imported homebrew is edited only from the Rules library — 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22, 2026-10-25
- [packs] Formulas: our own small evaluator; never rolls; fractions round down and say so; unreadable formulas stay text — 2026-10-22
- [packs] Rule hooks (fill, warn, factor, remind) are data, never code, in the settled spec in 07 (fields, events, test language, fixed functions); the runner comes in M4b; warnings never block, GM fiat wins — 2026-10-21, 2026-10-22
- [packs] Pack notes are up to three short sentences in your own words (setting and homebrew packs; adventure packs aim to be complete, 2026-10-24); new kinds are JSON only, Markdown stays version 1 — 2026-10-18

**Play (later milestones)**
- [core] The app never rolls in R1; dice stay physical and results are typed in — 2026-10-18
- [xp] Level-up fills values from class progression; for each value the player edited, they choose the setting's or theirs — 2026-10-18
- [items] Equipping against the class's rules is blocked (the pop-up says why); gear that stops casting can be worn, with a derived "Can't cast" status; a two-handed weapon with a shield offers Stow the shield — 2026-10-21, 2026-10-25
- [expedition] Time is set as durations (defaults 6-second round, 10-minute turn, 24-hour day) and the app works out the ratios; one seconds counter underneath, one main advance per mode that runs the procedure (Advance by… for odd amounts), Set time… for corrections only; only the GM moves game time (no real time between sessions) — 2026-10-17, 2026-10-29
- [core] The full log is one tap from Events (built in M9) (confirmed 2026-10-22) — 2026-10-18
- [perm] Secret ballots are hidden from everyone, the GM included; joining and title votes pass by simple majority, charter changes are unanimous — 2026-10-17, 2026-10-18


Older entries: `09a-decisions-archive.md`

## 2026-11-07
- 2026-11-07 — [packs] A creation-slot table may carry `forRaces: [raceIds]` (G-203, a): it is offered to that kindred after its own `tables` (both show, the bound one "Optional, from <pack>"; Design, same day) and displaces the unbound slot tables (ct-5) for the slots it fills; a table bound to other kindreds isn't offered; an unknown race id is a `pack:check` note (07, Table uses registry) — Francis's homebrew tables pack gives kindreds their own names and details from an overlay

## 2026-11-04
- 2026-11-04 — [items] Weapon rules and class forms as built (area 3 order 10; eq-7, eq-8, mb-4): a `weaponRules` category is matched against an item's weapon tags or its `category`, without regard to case, the pack on top first; a class on `exceptClasses` or with no `weaponProficiencyCategory` follows its own list; a character who never switched is in the first form the class lists; the current form lives in `character_play.form_id` (`character.setForm`, simple log), and the gear the new form can't use comes off in the same change, carried — The ruling named the shapes; these are the builder's readings of what it left open, harmless where the pack writes the rules as the zine overlay does.
- 2026-11-04 — [process] Until Thursday: everything the M5b gate needs built and live, one gate list (docs/testing.md, the gate run); pack review areas 4–7 after, their rulings planned in their own milestone and built there unless they fix something in play (Francis, A) — keeps the M5b gate achievable
- 2026-11-04 — [expedition] Every place is Neither, Safe or Hostile (Safe or Hostile makes a settlement); between expeditions only the clock moves; parts of the day in thirds from 06:00; Split evenly asks how many watches when the pack doesn't say (`design/rulings/m5b-builder-questions-3-2026-11-04.md`, bq3-1, bq3-2, bq3-4) — settlements unblocked, town checks belong to expeditions
- 2026-11-04 — [packs] Patches in detail (dotted paths, delta and times, the pack on top, after byOption, removed gear kept by name "Off"); treasure is what the rules set names; chosen magic armour equips if a base fits and the GM picks; a background's spell roll sits under its pick (bq3-5, bq3-8, bq3-9, bq3-13, bq3-17 … bq3-19) — the builders' open questions closed
- 2026-11-04 — [items] Equipment review: magic items carry `baseId`; race limits checked in play; treasure suggests "carrying treasure", the GM decides; the pack on top wins a shared item id, shown; containers weigh by fill; carry bonuses; weapon rules by combat category; two-form classes; quick-kit rolls by class (`design/rulings/m5b-pack-review-area-3-2026-11-04.md`, eq-1 … eq-9) — the books' gear rules without hand work, and nothing silently overridden
- 2026-11-04 — [process] The builders' technical questions of 2026-11-04 settled as the builder recommended (Francis; `design/rulings/m5b-builder-questions-3-2026-11-04.md`) — builder calls, not design
- 2026-11-04 — [sheet] The full character sheet: captioned Combat tiles, one Movement section in the pack's words, skills in one place, traits by source, a Magic section, Advancement with an XP bar, fields only when they apply, two columns on a tablet, the to-hit row under descending AC, a load bar per band; `sheetFields` with a checker as the model for every setting (`design/rulings/m5b-sheet-review-2026-11-04.md`, sr-1 … sr-10) — every printed field on the sheet or one tap away, and no setting can drop one silently

## 2026-11-03
- 2026-11-03 — [ui] Actions are buttons and links only go somewhere, everywhere; the Encounter waiting card's View and Dismiss become small buttons; the Watches step is laid out by watch with + Add and Split evenly (`design/rulings/m5b-fewer-links-2026-11-03.md`, fl-1 … fl-4) — Francis: "I want fewer links", and the watch chips per character read poorly
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] An option gate may list several choices (`byOption` keys too); an overlay may carry patches that change or remove a base pack's matched entries under its option, never editing the base (`design/rulings/m5b-options-format-2-2026-11-03.md`, of-12, of-13) — the zine's materials and energy weapons change the base's items
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] A race's `byOption` may change any field, ability modifiers included; a hard limit refuses at Pick with an explaining toast; the location view's phone sheet readings confirmed (`design/rulings/m5b-builder-questions-2-2026-11-03.md`, bq2-1 … bq2-3) — one rule for option-changed fields, and never a disabled button
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] A kit item the class can't use is taken carried, never worn or held, and labelled at the Take; an empty spell level never blocks Next (of-2); the seed buys a typical starting set in shop campaigns (`design/rulings/m5b-seed-input-2026-11-03.md`, si-1 … si-3) — nothing silently lost, nothing breaking class rules, and every seeded character testable
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] Options that behave: an overlay extends a base option's choices; one switch per rule, owned by the book that defines it; `option` and `byOption` on any entry through one shared reader, `null` removing a section; fields hidden (value kept) when their option is off; `forRaces`; modifiers behind an option; safe paths without a roll; any list level; distinct trait rolls; one weapon-damage rule; pick lines in starting equipment (`design/rulings/m5b-options-format-2026-11-03.md`, of-1 … of-11) — the audit found options working on some screens and not others
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] Settlement fields: services with a kind and rate, availability by size, the sale rule, a town mode stepping by part of day; one lodging rule (only Somewhere else… needs the GM) in and out of downtime (`design/rulings/m5b-settlement-switches-2026-11-03.md` amendments, st-2, st-3) — the town screen and requests can read the pack instead of text
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] Settlements stay in M5b, planned as step 6 (Francis B on the builder's scope check); the town switches come first: the GM sets Out and about today and Active tonight from the strip's ⋯, both reset at the next morning and at Start downtime, logged with Undo, players see only the state (`design/rulings/m5b-settlement-switches-2026-11-03.md`, sw-1 … sw-4) — the checks gated by `onlyWhen` had nothing to read
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] Kit rolls allow repeats unless `distinct: true`; every worked-out figure is tappable wherever it appears; the sheet's Movement follows the load like travel; a CON-based race save bonus shows beside the save, not added in (`design/rulings/m5b-builder-questions-2026-11-03.md`, bq-1, bq-2, bq-5, bq-8) — one pattern everywhere, and the sheet never disagrees with travel
- 2026-11-03 — [process] Tests first on a fix branch: commit the failing test as `test.fails`, then the fix removes `.fails`; the guard allows that edit (bq-6) — the old rule couldn't pass the pre-commit check without a banned bypass
- 2026-11-03 — [ui] The location view's phone sheet and "Where are we? · Set place" are built before the M5b gate; lr-9's Set time is dropped as covered by Set start time › (bq-4, bq-7) — the gate checks every screen at 375 px
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] Area-2 gaps: slot encumbrance in two bands (slowest wins); a row's next table by terrain; modifiers that set a chance's faces; a skill taking the best of the general and the class's own; water, flying, spoilage and special roads in R2, day-food and camp facts in M5b, fight food in M6, spell light in M7 (`design/rulings/m5b-pack-review-area-2-gaps-2026-11-03.md`, ga-1 … ga-5) — the books' exploration rules expressible without hand work
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] Exploration review: table chaining matched normalised (G-114 closes); party-keyed modifiers and `who: self`; a skill list where the best counts; optional rules off unless the book recommends; errata then the subject's home volume; budgets in points unless a point is a mile; options not yet applied shown as "Run by hand" (`design/rulings/m5b-pack-review-area-2-2026-11-03.md`, ex-1 … ex-7) — the packs can say what the books say, and nothing passes for working when it isn't
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] PR H's two technical choices, both B (Francis): a sheet layout field may carry `atCreation: false` (creation never asks it; it stays on the sheet), replacing the app's `NOT_ASKED_AT_CREATION` list; and the movement fill reads only the rules set's own mode key (no `movement.explore` copy of `exploration`): a layout names the mode as its rules set does — the pack says what it means; the app keeps no lists of a pack's words

- 2026-11-03 — [packs] `primeXp` takes the packs' shape (standard, or rules with a threshold per ability; the lowest prime decides by rules set) and the app changes; trait-list entries may give coins and a random spell; items by id, or a one-off by name; limits hard by the book's "must", soft by its "uncommon" (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-recheck-2026-11-03.md`, rc-1 … rc-5) — no class showed its XP modifier, and the packs' shape says what the books say
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] Creation follows the books: kindred name and trinket tables, an optional Details step, kindred grants added to the class's on a Magic step, kindred-classes bound to their kindred (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-survey-2026-11-03.md`, cs-1, cs-2, cs-4) — the survey found the books' steps in the packs but not in the app
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] Class limits live on the race, hard or soft (soft = special approval), with class alignment limits and the knight's liege; races carry `byOption`; kit, language and bundle fields; AC and save bonuses counted (cs-3, cs-5, cs-6, cs-7) — one place to read, and the books' own limits enforced
- 2026-11-03 — [process] Every part of the rules gets the creation survey's review (full read, values by script, book × pack × app, passes until clean), and each review gets an independent re-check by the read-only `pack-reviewer` agent in a fresh session (`docs/plans/pack-review-method.md`) — the pack drives everything, so no room for error or missed rules
- 2026-11-03 — [process] The review order is exploration and time, equipment and encumbrance, fights and monsters, magic and advancement, settlements, hirelings and downtime, then adventures; every new pack conversion follows the same method and isn't handed over until the `pack-reviewer` re-check is reconciled — in-use areas first, and new packs must meet the same standard
- 2026-11-03 — [ip] Private packs may keep the books' exact wording; the 8-word copying check is information only — the packs are the owner's private, non-commercial copies and rules must be exact (a paraphrase can change a rule); the repo rules are unchanged: no book text, numbers or real names in the repo, and `packs/` is never committed
- 2026-11-03 — [ip] The in-app "Convert with an AI" instructions follow the same ruling: keep the rules in the book's exact wording, numbers as printed (07 appendix) — the owner converts their own books for private use
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] A place's picture reaches players only when the GM shows the picture itself, with its own eye like a piece; showing a piece no longer reveals it. It is a piece of its own kind (shown per visit, hidden by Hide all, brought back on a return visit, one group's only) (m5b-1 follow-up ⚑, ruled B by Francis; `plans/m5b-1-travel.md`, Departures) — 05's "only once sent or revealed", and no second show system
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] Third creation test (fae-woodland fighter): tiles 3 + 3 in landscape; Affiliation on the sheet, not asked; two rolls on one table pick two rows; every worked-out figure tappable (the "How it's worked out" link goes); every pack-list value opens its entry (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-test-3-2026-11-03.md`, ct3-2, ct3-3, ct3-6, ct3-9, ct3-11) — explain in place, never a number or a word without its why
- 2026-11-03 — [characters] The book's full creation (names, glamours, trinkets, appearance rolls, kindred abilities) waits for a pack survey of the creation chapter, then a design session (ct3-12) — the gap is mostly the pack and the app not reading what the book has
- 2026-11-03 — [design system] A full-screen pop-up closes with the same Close ✕ at the right of its bar as every other pop-up; the Back ‹ that closed it is gone. A Back ‹ stays only where it goes back a step inside the full screen (a table opened from Which table? or Tables ›); there the ✕ and Escape close the whole thing (tf-8's scope check, ruled B by Francis; `plans/m5b-test-feedback-2026-11-03.md`, part 1) — one way to close everywhere
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] A group that forced the march and comes to Next day without a camp that evening gets the forced march's rest applied as unanswered: Push on, the pack's condition, stacked, the same as any unanswered rest; Undo of Next day reverses it. With a camp, camp's Sleep step flow applies as before (camp follow-up ⚑, ruled B by Francis; `plans/m5b-4-camp.md`, Departures) — a forced march always costs its rest
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] Camp's scope checks (Francis; `plans/m5b-4-camp.md`, Departures): a camp step's part (fire, cooking, talk, eat) is read from its kind or id, a missing part placed by D13, no 07 field (SC-4-1 A); watches are tap chips, up to 3 per watch, not hold-to-drag (SC-4-2 A); a forced march's rest falls due in camp's Sleep step, "Rest due (forced march)", and Force march no longer opens the rest prompt at once (SC-4-3 A; replaces the m5b-1 workaround); camp's technical choices 1–14 confirmed — chips meet 44 px and the screen-reader rules with no drag library, and players answer the rest where they already are

- 2026-11-03 — [packs] A choice name of up to 14 letters counts as its short label: On / Off and other short names stay a segmented control without `short`; pack:check notes only a 2–3 choice option that would fall back to the ▾ button (Francis, part 2 Departures item 1, reading A) — the existing On / Off rows keep working before the packs add labels

- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] Camp's three option rules (smoking for clues, inebriation, falling asleep on watch; m5b-4 build order 8) may slip past the M5b gate; whether they do is decided at the gate (`plans/m5b-4-camp.md` ⚑ 1) — the largest M5b step, kept to its core first
- 2026-11-03 — [ui] Selected states use a pale accent wash with an accent border, ink text and ✓, everywhere (two new tokens); one Close, a bare ✕ top right (`design/rulings/m5b-test-feedback-2026-11-03.md`, tf-4 A, tf-8 B) — black fills read as pressed and competed; a text-link Close was hard to find
- 2026-11-03 — [library] Rules rows keep their place while you work; up to 3 short choices as a segment, more as a ▾ button with "1 of N"; no Show all; shared rules list every pack's choices; the App section opens campaign creation's rules step; no "Both" choice (tf-1 A, tf-2 A, tf-3 C, tf-5 B, tf-6 B, tf-7 A) — the GM sees what's chosen and what else there is, without the list moving
- 2026-11-03 — [ui] The GM reference: a 220 px left list with two headings instead of the "pack" tag; values in words (tf-10 A, tf-11) — the content needs the width
- 2026-11-03 — [core] Fifth deletion exception: undoing an action that created an item (an activity's yield) removes it while untouched, the log keeps a copy; Undo is refused once any is used (`design/rulings/m5b-3-yield-undo-2026-11-03.md`, A; #312 ⚑ A) — Undo must work the same everywhere, and an untouched yield never really entered play
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] "Show hex numbers to players" (S4-6) moves to M8b with the hex grids — there is nothing to show before then (m5b-1 ⚑ 1)
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] The location view's picture is a campaign homebrew image uploaded from the GM's device in M5b; Drive images come in M8b (m5b-1 ⚑ 2)
- 2026-11-03 — [data] Per-group wilderness state lives in two tables: `travel_groups` (the day's budget, spent, forced marches, the shortfall owed, the ground around the group) and `group_lost` (Lost until Found the way, with the recovery roll); the GM reads all, a player their own group's row; `group_lost` reaches a player only while Getting lost is Open (m5b-1, build order 1) — hidden means hidden, enforced by row-level security
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] Travel log lines are the GM's (`travel.*`); players read the budget itself; Force march and the recovery roll are a player's commands (m5b-1) — the log stays the GM's, players act on their own group
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] Until camp's sleep step exists (m5b-4), a forced march opens the group's shared rest flow at once (a called rest, g6), applied by the GM's Next day: that is "rest due that evening" for now (m5b-1 workaround) — replaced 2026-11-03 (SC-4-3 A, above): the rest is due in camp's Sleep step
- 2026-11-03 — [expedition] The Lost card's chance is the pack's lost check for the group's terrain (07 has no separate recovery field); each morning until found (S2-5) (m5b-1)

- 2026-11-03 — [packs] A spell list may `extends` another class's list behind an option (with `levelShift`); built in M7 with spell books (`design/rulings/m5b-packs-g80-g06-2026-11-03.md`, pg-1, G-80 A) — an optional rule moves a supplement's spells to an existing caster without hand edits
- 2026-11-03 — [packs] A class may carry `raceMaxLevels`, merged into the races' `classMaxLevels` (the race wins a clash); built in M5b as a creation follow-up, the rest of G-06 stays after R1 (pg-2, G-06 race side A) — an add-on's class can reach the base pack's races without patching them

## 2026-11-02
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] Downtime is a section in a homebrew or overlay pack with the survey's 11 changes (formulas, band effects, trouble kinds, progress options, targets, free activities, grants, settlement money); the app reads it in three stages and shows unread fields to the GM as text (`design/rulings/m7-downtime-format-2026-11-02.md`, dt-46) — the pack converts once, the app catches up
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Secret trackers show reached steps and "? · the GM knows" (dt-47); natural-dice bands get a chip (dt-48); troubles have a deadline, block or repeat (dt-49); free activities take no action slot (dt-50) — the supplement's shapes without breaking the downtime rulings
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Arriving at a safe town raises an arrival card for the GM (End expedition, then Start downtime: two taps); the split and Award XP belong to End expedition; Back in safety / Into town retired (`design/rulings/m7-downtime-2026-11-02.md` dt-2, dt-3, dt-9) — the party reaches town by Where? or travel, and may set out again at once
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] One significant action per character per downtime plus any number of upkeep actions; helping uses the helper's action (Pass with a note) (dt-1, dt-23) — structure without limiting the narrative
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Activities: the player proposes (pack activity or Other, in their words), the GM may ask, then sets terms; the player approves or sends back; ongoing ones come first and advance by their rules, by time, or by Advance / Pause (dt-16 … dt-18, dt-27) — the back-and-forth is the heart of downtime
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Progress steps (trackers) move from R2 into R1, shown as segmented bars like the HP bar with the current and next step beneath (dt-19, dt-26, dt-33) — the sources make the tracker central; screens stay visual
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] The player rolls and taps the band on a table they can see; the result applies by itself and goes to the GM to approve and colour; bonuses are asked for with a reason (dt-20, dt-21, dt-32, dt-34) — fewer taps, the GM keeps the story
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Upkeep results may be fixed, narrative, a table or set by the rules, once per period; some upkeep can't be skipped (skip = worst result, Disband instead) (dt-37, dt-38, dt-41) — running a business has consequences
- 2026-11-02 — [expedition] Downtime moves by any amount and every Due multiplies; players get a one-time summary card; troubles carry clocks in downtimes or game time and stay private to their player and the GM (dt-24, dt-25, dt-31, dt-35, dt-36) — the app remembers deadlines the books leave to the GM
- 2026-11-02 — [items] Lodging (shared stays, four ways to pay), stored places as one kind (bank, vault, strongbox; items too), pack fees on Due, the GM's ad-hoc fees, Spend… with a visible note and Hide, auto-pay from a purse or the treasury (the Treasurer approves), unpaid dues covered by borrowing, loans, selling or credit; costs run during expeditions (dt-8, dt-11 … dt-15, dt-28 … dt-30, dt-39, dt-40) — town life without paperwork
- 2026-11-02 — [core] Notes and information: a party sheet per company (Company page, Scene shortcut) and each player's own sheet; writer and session a tap away; the writer, Archivist and GM edit; the GM sends rumours and information; kinds for all; Share with the party; unknown items in the pack's Unidentified section, identified with the existing Reveal (dt-4 … dt-7, dt-10, dt-42 … dt-45) — leads live with the players, the GM reads them
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] Encounter table rows may carry `statBlockIds` and Add foes adds the whole row at one band (`design/rulings/m5b-gaps-g68-g70-2026-11-02.md`, G-68 A) — a rival party is met as one encounter; the table already exists
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] An item whose study grants a reward goes to the downtime design session with training and projects (G-69 A) — study is a downtime activity; its rewards are ruled with the rest
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `gmOnly` may hide one custom field by its label; the importer honours it, the checker warns on an unknown label (G-70 A) — 07 promised it; a book's catalogue can show while its hiding place stays the GM's
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] A class change in `creation.redo` also clears the hit points and walks Who they are again (B; #282) — the hit die is the class's
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] The `ruleKey` group pick is built as its own step (#283; 07 had said M4b in error); a losing pack's own `byOption` keys and gates read that pack's own choice (A) — the callout was display-only until the resolution existed
- 2026-11-02 — [library] Deleting a pack removes it from the campaigns using it (as 07 says; lr-3's "keeps a copy" withdrawn); the Rules tab stays one stacked page without chips, and the app's own settings keep today's rows (A; #279, #285) — the mockups overstated what was built
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Every pick row ends in a Pick → Picked ✓ button, the row's text opens the entry, and on a one-of-many step Pick moves on at once (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-test-2-2026-11-02.md`, ct-1; supersedes cr-5's ✓ slot) — Francis's second test: the empty square said nothing
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Entries carry six headings with bullet advantages and open classes as chips (ct-2) — the one-block entry was hard to read
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Adjust scores shows only the tradeable scores, names the ones kept as rolled, and lists each trade as a chip with ✕ (ct-3; supersedes cr-11's Undo per trade and answering taps) — the Undo icon and the dotted tiles were ambiguous
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Derived fields shows only fields with a value, AC with its parts named; the hit-points box carries the arithmetic and one line, no prompt; tile words name the thing (ct-4, ct-5) — blank tiles and "2-in-6" told the player nothing
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] The kit picks first and its rolls unfold under it; a spell book's entry is one row per spell; weapon proficiencies are a pick list with the count and specialisation (ct-6 … ct-8) — the built order forced the rolls first, the panel ran spells together, the field asked for typing
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] A creation-relevant option appears when on and is absent when off, with a test per option and the pack tying each to data (ct-10) — the test found four options ignored and more unmodelled
- 2026-11-02 — [places] A piece is edited by tapping its text: the Add piece sheet filled in, Save, one undoable command; a shown piece that changes updates quietly for players (`design/rulings/m5b-g2b-pieces-follow-up-2026-11-02.md`, gp-1, gp-2) — Francis ruled pieces editable at the g2 merge; a correction should read as if it had always said so
- 2026-11-02 — [places] Removing a piece retires it (the `undone` flag; Undo brings it back), never deletes it (gp-3) — the closed list of deletions stays closed
- 2026-11-02 — [places] A return to a place (same place, any group, any expedition) starts with the pieces shown at the end of its last visit; hidden ones stay hidden, new ones wait for the eye; one log line with Undo (gp-5) — Francis ruled it at the g2 merge: the party knows the room
- 2026-11-02 — [places] `place.show` and `place.hide` retired: unregistered, their two `logged_commands` rows deleted (Francis OK'd in plain words), their earlier log lines still render (gp-6) — nothing has called them since g2
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] The `ruleKey` group resolution (07, 2026-10-24/25) is built now as its own step (PR #281's scope check, ruled option B plus A's doc fix): one choice per group, the GM's pick kept in the campaign's `settings.ruleGroups`, default from the pack order, "New choice for <topic>" on attaching (the standing choice pinned until the GM confirms), fall-back on removing (logged) — the plan and 07 had called it built in M4b; it wasn't, and lr-6's "one choice counts" wasn't true in play
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] A pack whose rule lost its `ruleKey` group keeps reading its own stored choice in its own `byOption` keys and gates; the group pick decides only the topic's effective choice for the campaign (`groupChoice`) — Francis, superseding the first reading on #283's scope check (the winner's choice carried onto the loser), which read a pack's book against a choice it never offered
- 2026-11-02 — [library] The Rules library is grouped by kind with chips, sorted by name, each pack in four lines (description under the name, what it holds, who uses it) with Browse and Update on the row and Delete under ⋯ (`design/rulings/m5b-0d-library-and-campaign-rules-2026-11-02.md`, lr-1 … lr-3) — one flat list told a GM nothing about what a pack held or where it was used
- 2026-11-02 — [navigation] The landscape column folds from its divider (a quiet pill, tap or drag), nothing in the column (lr-4) — the chevron row and "Fold the menu" looked wrong in the menu
- 2026-11-02 — [campaign] Set-up steps 2 and 3 move onto the creation step shell: packs by kind with the ✓ slot and "needs <pack> ✓"; optional rules one folded group per source with source, page and when it can change in words; a ruleKey clash as one callout (lr-5, lr-6) — the same shell everywhere, and the clash visible instead of two rows asking the same thing
- 2026-11-02 — [campaign] Rules for this campaign folds its topic groups with count lines and holds the optional rules as one group per source, editable in place with Undo; Reset to the books' defaults replaces Use all defaults (lr-7) — one place, one look
- 2026-11-02 — [campaign] The Rules tab stays one stacked page (Rules for this campaign, then Setting rules; no chips), and the app's own settings keep today's rows inside the folded topic groups (Francis, 0d.4's scope checks, both A) — the short page wasn't worth a navigation change, and new rows would need new words
- 2026-11-02 — [campaign] Retire campaign… sits at the foot of Rules for this campaign (lr-8) — it is a campaign setting, not a pack action
- 2026-11-02 — [scene] The time control reads Set time until the campaign's time is first set (lr-9) — "Advance" before anything is set made no sense
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `hitPoints.reroll { atMost, option?, note? }` drives the Roll again / Keep it callout on Who they are (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-followups-2026-11-02.md`, cf-1) — the trigger was prose in both settings; same pattern as `scores.startOver`
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `hitPoints { startMinimum?, minimumPerDie?, reroll? }` named in 07 (cf-2) — both packs already write them; the checker can validate and M7 reads the second
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] No Decide later on Who they are (cf-3) — the name makes the draft and the GM never approves a character without hit points
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `startingGear.shop` (through `byOption`) hides the shop; `startingEquipment` is a Given group on Gear (cf-4) — the app keeps no option ids, and the items everyone gets had no step
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] Sheet layouts are player-readable (cf-5) — per-race lists and field option gates live there and a layout holds nothing secret
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] A typed Other… stays on the character; the GM's review offers Add to homebrew (cf-6) — players never write homebrew, the GM stays the gate
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] `creation.redo` lets an unapproved draft change scores, race or class; a changed race or class clears the later picks with Undo and walks them again (cf-7) — "start a new character" after a change of mind is the paperwork the app exists to remove
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] A class change at creation also clears the typed hit points and walks Who they are again for a new roll (name and alignment kept); a race change alone and scores-only changes keep them (follow-up C, ⚑ ruled B, amended that evening) — the hit die is the class's, so the old roll no longer means anything
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] A table may say `"gmOnly": false` and campaign members read it; the class's starting-spell tables carry it (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-first-spells-table-2026-11-02.md`, fs-1) — a player rolling first spells must see the ranges; the pack names the table, so the rule reads one flag instead of every class, and the rows aren't copied onto the class
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] A table row's `spells: [ids]` is the one field a roll's spells come from (fs-2) — one reader, no name matching
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] `firstSpells.rollTable` may list several tables, one roll each (fs-3) — a setting's caster rolls on two tables; the mockup's "twice" on one table is withdrawn
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] The score tiles start at the dice average rounded down (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-amendments-2026-11-02.md`, ca-1) — a half never shows on a tile; down is the conservative start
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Race and Class have no Decide later (ca-2) — every later step needs the class and the race drives the class list
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Ask the GM to allow a race or class is replaced: the row says "Needs the GM's special approval", the player picks it and keeps going, and the GM's approval screen flags the choice (ca-3; supersedes cr-7) — one flow for approval instead of a Request mid-creation; the `allowChoice` kind is retired
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `creationGuide.<step>.title` names a step in the pack's word (ca-4) — the shell showed "Kindred" with no field to read it from
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] A guided draft reopened from Your characters opens on the creation review, not the step it stopped on (cr-18: Save and leave resumes at the review; m5b-0c slice 7) — the review re-checks every step on Send and lands on the first that fails, so nothing is skipped
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Next on Gear goes on with no starting money typed; the purse then starts at 0 (slice 5's ⚑, `plans/m5b-0c-creation-rebuild.md`) — cr-14 has Next check only overspending, and a character may set out with nothing
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] An option that `requires` another is an add-on and carries no `ruleKey` (ca-5) — one choice per ruleKey group would have made the add-on unpickable beside its parent

## 2026-11-01
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Character creation is rebuilt step by step on one shell (`design/rulings/m5b-0c-creation-rebuild-2026-11-01.md`, cr-1 … cr-18; mockups `design/mockups/creation/`): Back keeps every value, Next checks and explains, a step exists only when the setting and the character call for it, the row opens an entry with Pick and the ✓ slot picks, Help me choose is about the choice, an unmet requirement offers Ask the GM — Francis's test found two visual styles and steps that asked for derived values or never enforced the setting's limits
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Scores: tiles from the dice average, modifier and effect live, the setting's start-over rule offered; no digital roll in creation ("Roll for me" parked); the pack must say the dice and range — dice stay at the table and the app never invents a range
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Race and class rows carry a fit line from the scores (requirement, prime requisite and XP bonus, hit die, the race's level cap) and a six-heading entry; classes not open to the race stay folded, and Ask the GM can open them — the consequence beside the choice, the book closed
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Adjust scores exists only when the rule is data: −2 / +1 / Undo per trade, a bank line, forbidden taps answer, reset on a class change, no Decide later — the old step never enforced the rule because the rule was prose
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Who they are asks name, hit points (the class's die, CON, the minimum, the re-roll option), alignment, portrait and free fields; everything the rules work out sits read-only under Derived fields, from the pack's `fill` marks only; alignment visibility is per pack — nothing derived is ever asked
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Gear: money roll, kits, Browse the shop as the Pack screen's Add item in creation mode (Gold to spend, Done shopping), kit and shop together, unusable items listed and explained, the load tier live, a rolled-gear mode — the shop players know, not a second one
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Languages: the given ones (common, the alignment tongue that follows the alignment, race, class) plus N from INT from the setting's list, an Other… row, skipped when nothing is to pick; trait lists roll-then-tap or pick with Other… and a note; the review is read-only with Change ›, To finish, Send to the GM and Save and leave — one place to change a thing
- 2026-11-02 — [data] A character's alignment lives in its own table, `character_alignments`, moved out of `actors.fields` by a trigger and read as the setting's `alignmentVisibility` allows (its owner and the GM, or everyone) — a row rule can't hide one key inside a field (m5b-0c slice 1)
- 2026-11-02 — [packs] `creationGuide.<step>.title` names a step in the pack's word ("Kindred"); the shell uses it, the app's title when it's absent; `pack:check` accepts it
- 2026-11-02 — [characters] Starting hit points above level 1 add the HP modifier once per level (one die per level: level 3 with +1 adds +3); the roll typed is the level's hit dice, checked against their range (`plans/m5b-0c-creation-rebuild.md`, slice 3 item 4) — the modifier follows every die rolled
- 2026-11-02 — [ui] Score tiles on a phone sit two per row with 4 px between −, the number and + (each still 44 px): the score tiles' one exception to the 8 px between tap targets (PR D's ⚑, ruled B; the controls' line has no side padding)
- 2026-11-02 — [ui] The ✓ slot on a creation pick row sits on the left (cr-5; 06's Option row line brought in line); the below-the-minimum override dialog stays gone, the special-approval flag replaces it
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Creation rules as data (07): `scores` (required), `abilityAdjustment` as an object, `languages` with a numeric `extraLanguages`, `alignmentVisibility`, `fill` as the only derived mark, abilities' `kind` — the app enforces only what a pack states; both test packs move these from prose
- 2026-11-01 — [roadmap] First spells, the spell book list and the spell rules pop-up move from M7 into m5b-0c (`plans/m5b-0c-creation-rebuild.md`, ~70–95 turns in testable slices); the builder pauses other creation work — creation is what players see first and Francis wants it complete before the next test
- 2026-11-01 — [library] Edit pack stays for homebrew-kind packs; other packs keep the per-entry Edit as homebrew (library intent ⚑, ruled A) — a book's copy shouldn't be edited in place
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] The first day's start time (B13, g1's scope check, option A): the GM sets it in Rules for this campaign, Time and calendar (default 06:00); saving it moves a clock that hasn't moved yet (Day 1, no expedition, no advances) to that time, logged as one change with the setting; once the clock has moved it only shows, and the time changes with Advance by… → To a time; no display offset — an offset would put the app's days out of step with the database's day-based rules
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] While the clock hasn't moved, the GM's strip shows a quiet **Set start time ›** (like Set date ›) that opens the same start-time form and runs the same `time.start` command (g1b's scope check, option A) — so a new campaign doesn't sit at 00:00 just because the GM never opened Rules; nothing moves by itself
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] A check or rest due exactly as the hour turns over shows ahead on that hour's last turn, at the edge of the hour track (B8, g1's ⚑ 2, option B) — on the last turn the GM would otherwise not see the next hour's first check coming
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] The rules set's `attack.damageScore` names the ability score whose modifier adds to weapon damage (C3, g1's ⚑ 3, option A; 07); weapons at hand show it ("+1 · 1d8+1"), absent means the die alone as before, and `pack:check` accepts it — the attack bonus already read the scores, the damage had no field saying which one adds
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] `camp.cooking` `{ ingredients?, portions, bonus?, note? }` is final (engine reconcile ⚑ 1) — the draft engine and the pack already use it
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] A forced march makes rest due for the group that evening, answered in camp's sleep step through the shared rest flow (engine reconcile ⚑ 2) — players answer where they already are, not through a reminder of its own
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] A split party's groups never pay each other's shortfall or forced marches (engine reconcile ⚑ 3) — budget, shortfall and forced march are per group (2026-10-31)
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Weather tags' old `effect` stays an alias of `note` (engine reconcile ⚑ 4) — harmless, and the private packs' next candidates may still carry it
- 2026-11-01 — [process] The M5b engine draft (#169) is reconciled by plan, not rewritten (`plans/m5b-engine-reconcile.md`): rebase, six fixes (sleep `result`, one weather-tag reader, the pending place id, rest due through g6, the `option` alias, `speedOf`), drop its checker changes, merge as m5b-1's first commit — its behaviour still matches the wilderness rulings; only the code around it moved
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Rules overlay packs (G-55): a pack with `meta.requires` and no rules set may carry any kind; `gmReference` sits at the pack's top level or on a rules-set entry and the reference switcher merges them; `meta.sources[]` records which part of a growing source each thing came from — one source grows by issue, and the panels must read as the pack's whatever entry holds them
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] A table row may carry `tableId` beside its own `result` (G-42): the GM taps the row to roll the sub-table; M8b — the row is the target (B2), and a player may hear the row's text without the GM's variants
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Trait-list entries may grant gear (`items`, the kit shape) and change the spell list (`spells: add, replace`); kit and entry quantities may be dice and may name a required pack's item (G-48, G-57, G-62) — one shape for everything that hands out items, and a cult is a pick with consequences, not a kind
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Spells known rather than slotted (G-49, G-63): `spellcasting.known` with a count by level, uses per day and list level 0; a class may have slots, known or both; M7 — cantrips and runes are the same idea
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Items that apply a condition (`items[].applies`, G-53; onset and notice as text, the damage pad offers the status) and damaged items as conditions with `appliesTo: item` (G-52) — one effects vocabulary; a timer for onset is gold-plating
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] `locations` are allowed in rules and overlay packs with region optional (G-58); the drop-in flow is the prep-space design session's — an inn is a settlement piece, not a new kind
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Stat blocks get `variants` (sizes; Add foe at M6) and `parts` (hit-point pools; the screen is an M6 follow-up) (G-59, G-60) — format now so the pack is written once
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] `progression[].armourClass` feeds the AC line and `progression[].extra` shows labelled lines; powers known use `known` (G-61); `armour.tier` names an `armourType` the class may wear (G-65) — one computed column and one generic display column last longer than a column per class
- 2026-11-01 — [roadmap] Level 0 characters and the GM's Graduate at the first XP award are built in M7 (G-50); Award XP "as the treasure was split" in M7 (G-56) — both sit where levels and XP are built
- 2026-11-01 — [roadmap] Item materials (G-51), point-buy skills (G-66) and item-count encumbrance (G-67) have their format in 07 now and are built after R1 — nobody at the table uses them yet; homebrew items carry material numbers meanwhile
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Downtime projects (G-54) wait for the downtime design session with G-32; the two-class daily-switching race (G-64) is out of scope and stays text — one is the session's to design, the other a half-feature that would mis-compute the sheet
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] Zine-compendium confirmations: poison families by route (two families, not one "Poisoned"); cantrips at list level 0 on both lists and runes on their own list, no trait-list fallback; `appearance` on the adventure packs waits for M8b; the four maps stay unconverted until the B1 real-rooms test; the river adventure's toad points at the base pack's nearest block — B6 groups by family, so a family must stay short
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Trait-list steps at creation: **Now**, as M5b step 0b part B on Fable (`design/rulings/m5b-0-weapon-skills-and-traits-2026-11-01.md` §4, its ⚑ 1): `traitLists` becomes a library kind players read (row-level security through `private.player_facing`), a sheet field keeps its `from`, one step per list the fields point at (Decide later onto To finish under the list's id, `creation.require` with "traits" or the list's id), and the owner changes the pick from the sheet any time, logged — the importer dropped trait lists from every pack until the kind existed
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Help me choose with no pack guidance shows the step's choices with their blurbs side by side; the link stays (M5b step 0, ⚑ 1; replaces "without any, the link isn't shown") — players still get a comparison when a pack has blurbs but no longer help
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] The steps after Who are they? (gear, languages, weapon skills, traits) follow the pack's order: the order its `creationGuide` names them in, then the app's (M5b step 0, ⚑ 2) — the pack author knows the book's order
- 2026-11-01 — [roadmap] The generators are downtime and prep tools first (the town/downtime generator first in line); use at the table comes later — prep is where the GM has time to edit what a generator assembles
- 2026-11-01 — [packs] A new private pack, zine-compendium: one rules overlay on classic-advanced built by kind from a six-issue zine and made to grow issue by issue (sources per issue, minor version bumps), plus four tiny adventure packs (zine-adventure-1…4); converted whole after the pack session's current job; level-0 characters and cantrips included — it carries the first real trait lists, poison families, town services and small rooms the design needs for its tests
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Two builder questions after the gate: the "in darkness" symbol is the time band's light slot reading "◑ No light" (symbol and words, per group on the GM's strip), built with B18 in g6; the town's buying "Something else…" stays as a Request under A5, renamed "Something not listed…" — the removed button's name must not survive on a kept control
- 2026-11-01 — [process] November runs on a larger plan: the Fable 35% weekly rule is off for the month, the builder uses Fable where a plan qualifies without asking — design is ahead and the build should catch up
- 2026-11-01 — [roadmap] Five generators parked in 08 (NPC and monster creator, treasure/trap/puzzle, dungeon room, hex, town): GM prep tools rolling and assembling from the packs' own tables into the campaign's homebrew, never inventing content; their release is set at the prep-space design session — prep, not narration, and nothing the pack doesn't carry
- 2026-11-01 — [roles] 03's status row split for C1: adding a status is the GM's (or a spell's or ability's); removing or stepping their own stays the player's — the gate ruled the add half only
- 2026-11-01 — [characters] Weapon skills at creation (M5b-0, ruled Now; `design/rulings/m5b-0-weapon-skills-and-traits-2026-11-01.md`): the setting's weapons the class may use, picked one tap each up to the class category's start count, specialisation as a toggle spending picks when its option is on, restricted weapons shown but never picked; the picks live on the character, grow with levels through the To finish card, the owner adds while any are unspent and the GM changes the rest; shown in the Skills block and read by M6's attack prompts for the penalty — weapons, not groups, because that is how the pack's rule is written; trait-list steps designed the same way, Now or Later ⚑
- 2026-11-01 — [fight] Second pass on the fight screens: an attack starts from the weapon at hand (its prompt carries that attack type's modifiers and foe chips with AC where revealed; a foe tapped on Scene pre-selects), More on a foe card is a button, morale is a pop-up over the screen, the players' Scene shows a foe's AC once revealed, No fight · back to Explore asks for an optional note (Only you / In the log), the Player tab's items are At hand with a Pack › link, End turn only in individual initiative — the modifiers depend on how you attack, so the weapon comes first
- 2026-11-01 — [fight] The Player tab in a fight holds everything personal (the attack line for the tapped foe, weapons with bonus and damage, spells with Cast, abilities and items with Use, End turn and Delay in individual initiative), hit points read-only since the GM records damage; a player tapping hit points on the Player screen sees the history, not a pad — one place to act, the Scene stays a view
- 2026-11-01 — [fight] The fight screens reviewed on the mockup (`design/rulings/m6-fight-screens-review-2026-11-01.md`): Fiat is the wizard hat; the sequence strip shows the pack's own steps with the current one in the display size and a glow; a morale trigger is a pop-up prompt; no Damage or heal button (tap the target, the panel holds the pad); End foes' / End players' turn by whose turn it is; We run is the players' only, foes run as Fled and Undo on End fight covers a chase; the players' Scene is a reference (Foes · Party, no You column; tap yourself for the Player screen) and the personal controls (attack line, weapons, Use, End turn, Delay in individual initiative) live on the Player tab; Surprise and Distance lose Skip and Distance defaults to the average; Reaction is a button opening its table; Add foes puts Encounters above Bestiary and never opens the keyboard — the GM's screen does the bookkeeping, the players' screen stays a view
- 2026-11-01 — [fight] Party damage is entered by the GM for now, from the character's or hireling's panel (supersedes M6-1's "the players' job", 2026-10-29); players never enter damage — one hand on the numbers while the table test is young; revisit after M6's gate
- 2026-11-01 — [fight] M6's screens inherit the M5 gate (`design/rulings/m6-gate-alignment-2026-11-01.md`): the Encounter tables row and no typed roll (B2; M6-10's "type the roll" goes), the players' fight on the table view (B18), ✉ and Reference in the header (B3, B4), B6's status pop-up on foe cards, weapon numbers in the You column (C3); the encounter panel's Distance stays a typed number (a number is the result, not a row) — one language across Explore and Fight, nothing re-ruled
- 2026-11-01 — [process] M5b re-planned after the gate: m5b-0 creation revisited first, then the gate build steps g1 (sweep) → g2–g5 (place pieces, encounter tables, messaging, GM reference; parallel allowed) → g6 (rest flow, players' table view, statuses), then the wilderness steps 1–5 with the old table pick folded into g3 and the players' wilderness screen built onto the g6 view; about 180–240 builder turns, M5b and M6 slip about two weeks — remove before adding, and the wilderness screen should be built once, on the redesigned view
- 2026-11-01 — [core] The M5 gate's open questions ruled: players never add statuses to their own characters (the GM, or a spell or ability, does); weapons at hand show attack bonus and damage now; a quiet "N waiting · Players ›" line on the GM's console leads to waiting Requests; on escape rolls a hireling leaves with its employer; the GM may enter a roll for a player without a device (logged as the GM's); months without a pack calendar are numbered, not named — each the lighter of the two choices, and none invents content the pack doesn't carry
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] The players' screen is one live table view (M5 gate B18): one sheet in three bands — time, marching order as tiles with the Rest button under them, then the place with its shown pieces — prompts in one slot below the time band, everything tappable for its explanation, no accent in the steady state — the player sees the table at a glance and is prompted when they must act instead of looking for things to do
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Statuses (M5 gate B6, ⚑ C2): a wider + Status pop-up with the durations on one row, "Until removed" replacing "None", the brief effect under every status name, families grouping the pack's related conditions behind one row (`conditions[].family`, G-46; families come only from the pack, the app invents none), and a custom status with a one-line What it does and a Stacks toggle instead of an effect builder — the GM reads what a status does without opening it, and the app never pretends to apply numbers
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] M5 gate small items B7–B17 ruled as noted at the gate: Resume session collapses a paused session and End session / End expedition explain themselves; due marks mean the turn being played; + Reminder with an icon per reminder; Keep lit as a pressed toggle; icons over the N, M, R letters (glyphs with the icon family); ⋯ reads Encounter check and Advance by… (Set time… folded in); a start time (default 06:00) and a day-and-month date without a pack calendar; procedure steps in plain words; a collapsible left menu on iPad landscape; the place card loses "Only you see this" and gets an expandable Notes; no "Revealed this downtime" card — small frictions the live gate found, none needing a mockup
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] Rest is a shared flow (M5 gate B5): the strip counts down to it for everyone; on the due step each player answers Rest / Push on on their own screen and the GM's panel shows what each did (no buttons per row, tap a row to flip it, Everyone rests / No one rests, Minimise); Next turn applies it, unanswered count as Push on, one log line is the record; a pushed-on character keeps "Rest due" until they rest; a player's Rest button under the marching order calls a rest out of turn; the Procedure button goes and the procedure opens by tapping the mode and turn on the strip — the players are prompted to act instead of looking for the button, and the GM gets a record, not a chore
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] The GM reference screen (M5 gate B4): an open-book icon in the top row opens the book full screen — search, standard panels built from the pack (Combat, Dungeons with the common player actions, Class abilities, Equipment, Tables), extra panels from a new `gmReference` list on rules sets and adventures (G-45), a pack switcher when several packs are attached, every entry the pack's text with its page — a stand-in for the referee's paper screen, not tied to the GM's current screen; the Rules tab stays about the campaign
- 2026-11-01 — [core] Messaging (M5 gate B3) replaces Send, From the GM and send-to-GM: private threads between the GM and a player only (no player-to-player, no Everyone), a ✉ icon beside the log icon, attachments (handouts, the adventure's crops and clues in M8b, a file), one switch "Disappears once read" in place of keep-or-burn, a silent arrival card, Send with Undo — secrets go person to person, what everyone hears is said aloud, and the R3-audit line's "people-to-people" narrows to GM ↔ player
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] Encounter tables go full screen (M5 gate B2): a Hit opens one flat list (session pins, then the likely table first, then the rest; each row name · why · die · one-line description), tapping a row opens the table, tapping the rolled row is the result (logged, Undo); every row at every level carries a one-line description; column chips only when the setting's tables have columns; pins are per session, set in prep; packs gain `tables[].description` (G-44); the one-row "current table · Roll" and Enter roll… go — the GM reads and taps instead of typing, and prep (paper test due) carries the choice of tables
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] Show players goes piece by piece (M5 gate B1): every piece of a place (description, exits, people, clues, treasure, hazards) has its own 44 × 44 eye icon button at the row's right, no word or note (open eye + pressed look = shown, struck-through = hidden), Hide all, + Piece, Undo; a person shows its description never its stat line, treasure its text never its value or a magic item's hidden details, a hazard its look never its trigger; adventure packs gain `contents[].appearance` and `hazards[].appearance` (07 §2, G-43) — the GM reveals a room as the table reaches it, and secrets stay hidden by construction
- 2026-11-01 — [core] R3 audit of the 40 M5 features (`design/rulings/m5-r3-audit-2026-11-01.md`): the darkness dim goes (the "in darkness" symbol stays), Send and From the GM give way to people-to-people messaging (B3), the weather card's automatic fold goes, the place card's GM screen button folds into the GM reference screen (B4); everything else is kept or already on the redesign list — narration aids and decoration out, bookkeeping and rules lookup in
- 2026-11-01 — [testing] M5 gate run live (a GM and two players on iPads): every testable step passed; escape rolls untested until a pack carries a dungeon exit rule; the hand-off is `design/rulings/m5-gate-rulings-2026-11-01.md` — the gate passes with a redesign list, not a re-test
- 2026-11-01 — [process] M7-4's character-creation revisit moves into M5b and is built first, before the wilderness steps; first spells stay in M7; M5b and M6 slip about a week — creation is the players' first meeting with the app and must be right before the table test
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Players mostly use iPads too: the iPad layout comes first for players as well as the GM, phones stay supported, and "phone" no longer stands for "player" in 06 — the gate was played on three iPads
- 2026-11-01 — [core] Guiding principle (R3): the app speeds up bookkeeping, record-keeping and rules lookup so players get off screens and into narration; pop-ups and reminders only where someone might miss something or must act quickly; no narration aids beyond revealing descriptions and visual aids; the players' screen in play is a live table view with prompts — the paper test had this and the M5 build drifted; every M5 feature is audited (keep · simplify · remove) before M5b
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Never open the keyboard automatically, app-wide, unless a spec says otherwise — the gate found fields grabbing focus on open
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] Something else… and Describe what you want to do are removed, with the cost-in-turns answer (supersedes S4-2 and the 2026-10-31 Describe… ruling); the common player actions move to the GM reference screen; Requests keep escape rolls and the GM-answered mechanics — actions and costs are narrated, only time moves in the app, remote play has voice chat
- 2026-11-01 — [ui] Several turns pass by tapping Next turn again; the long-press +N control is dropped — it wasn't missed at the gate
- 2026-11-01 — [process] Parallel sessions coordinate through `docs/status/board.md` (claim · update at hand-off · re-read on "sync"), each in its own Git worktree, with a read-only Sonnet `coordinator` agent to report duplicates and blocks — sessions can't talk to each other, Francis is the bus, and orchestration never runs on Fable
- 2026-11-01 — [process] M5b split into five build steps (travel · encounters from checks · the players' screen and activities · camp · the gate as a 3-day trek), plans in `docs/plans/m5b-*.md`; weather, Hit / Miss, special seasons, darkness and Rest today are already built and are verified, not rebuilt — the only M5b work that was blocking the builder was the plans
- 2026-11-01 — [process] The artifact chain from the AI-native SDLC playbook: `docs/intents/` as the front door (Francis's words, one page), rulings, plans with a Departures section kept in the same pull request, pull requests reviewed against a root `REVIEW.md`, status reports with two numbers (first-pass merge, ⚑ raised) — build isn't the bottleneck, Francis's attention is; each stage starts from a committed file
- 2026-11-01 — [process] Automatic review of code pull requests in CI against REVIEW.md on an API key with a monthly cap, plus `claude -p` triage of failed runs and a CHANGELOG draft (plan `process-sdlc-1`); findings never merge or block on their own — Francis judges intent and risk from the summary instead of reading every diff
- 2026-11-01 — [testing] A hook blocks edits to test files on `fix-*` branches (the failing test is written first, on its own commit) — a fix must not pass by weakening its test
- 2026-11-01 — [process] CLAUDE.md kept under a page: roles, models, budget and the gate workflow move to `.claude/README.md`; a "Things Claude gets wrong" section grows one line per mistake a review flags twice — stale context costs every session
- 2026-11-01 — [process] CI review and triage settings (Francis, ⚑1–2 of `process-sdlc-1`): the key is `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in repository secrets with a [amount removed] monthly cap; the automatic review runs only on pull requests touching `src/` or `supabase/` (docs-only and Dependabot skipped), Sonnet by default and Opus when `supabase/` changes; the CHANGELOG draft is built now, as a manual workflow that pushes a branch for Francis to edit — docs pull requests already have `docs:check`, and the cap bounds the cost
- 2026-11-01 — [process] Not now: agent evals on config changes, managed settings, the 3σ maintenance loop, Slack on-call — a solo project with no production users; revisit at M10
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] In M5 a Describe what you want to do answer is a cost in turns (one clock advance, undoable with the answer) and a note; the optional chance and the item or status effect land with M5b's activities (Francis, ⚑1 of the step 12 plan) — the Request pattern ships without waiting for activities
- 2026-11-01 — [testing] The M5 gate's scripted journey test (a 10-turn mock delve) stays in step 12, not step 13 (Francis, ⚑2 of the step 12 plan) — the regression spine exists before the gate
- 2026-11-01 — [expedition] Silent choices in step 12 (count as ruled unless Francis objects at the gate): the GM's escape-roll preview starts at Out safely and the GM switches it, since the pack's save rule is words, not a number the app can compare; the GM answers Requests from Waiting for you on Players (06), with the Leave the dungeon toast pointing there; a party-wide escape roll (`who: party`) is read and entered by any player of the campaign; the GM never enters or cancels a player's request

- 2026-10-31 — [expedition] Describe what you want to do stays in the dungeon as a Request the GM costs in turns — turn modes have no activity buttons (DG12), but players still need a way to ask
- 2026-10-31 — [process] 31 choices the build agents made where the rulings were silent are confirmed as built: groups named "Group 1, 2…" with 1 the party; names in the players' marching strip until symbols; saved orders and the GM lock campaign-wide; any player may Join up until places per group exist; a joining group goes to the back; the GM's group in focus per device; a hireling's employer read as its owning player; weather due from an expedition's first day, the player's Undo 30 s in the database behind an 8 s toast, the GM enters the roll when no season is known, "The GM rolls today's weather." with nothing in the urgent strip, a tag with no note shows its tidied id; the Act menu GM-only; step 9's statuses and rest as built (05); the start place GM-only in its own table; a recent place picks the mode it started in; the compact time strip under the top row with Next turn secondary; Check now falling back to the GM's reminders and ignoring day-only / night-only; the gutter check one party roll after a split; every turn mode dark without a light, dark sight as any race with `vision`, the dim each time a group goes dark; the darkness switch in ⋯ › This device; Travelling in darkness staying on into the next expedition, day modes only — each is already built and harmless, so a yes costs nothing
- 2026-10-31 — [process] Scope: the settlement view's build (ST1–ST14, WA6) and its four open questions leave M5b for the downtime design session before M7 — M5b's risk was high and the questions are downtime questions
- 2026-10-31 — [ui] Join up is a quiet button beside the group chips (shown only when two groups share a place once places per group exist), not in the switcher's ⋯ — rejoining is the one action the switcher exists for
- 2026-10-31 — [data] In the wilderness the travel budget, carried-over cost, forced march, Lost and the camp are per group of a split party; the clock stays one for all — each group already has its own place, order and lights

- 2026-10-31 — [ui] The "Weather rolled by" setting moves to Rules › Adventuring, as session 4 ruled — with its siblings Getting lost and Show hex numbers

- 2026-10-31 — [ui] The darkness dim lowers the background; text and controls keep full contrast: for the 4 seconds the screen takes the dark theme's colours with darker backgrounds (in light mode the text turns light), switched at once both ways, never a see-through layer; dark sight keeps its grey fade — A lighter overlay either fails the contrast rule or stops reading as dark, and dark text can't keep contrast on a darkened background (B6; how, Francis 2026-10-31)
- 2026-10-31 — [packs] A fourth reference pack, byzantine-dungeon (a megadungeon with its own town and mini-setting), surveyed and fit-checked in stages; register rows G-25 to G-38 — it stresses exactly what M5, M5b, M7 and M8b model
- 2026-10-31 — [expedition] M5 step 10 confirmed as built: Where? keeps the start place with the expedition, GM only, until places per group (step 11); Choose from adventures › hidden until adventure packs (M8b); a recent place reopens in the mode it last started in, a typed place asks — both superseded by later steps, so a yes costs nothing

- 2026-10-30 — [expedition] "Which season?" makes today the chosen season's first day — The tap means "it's that season now"
- 2026-10-30 — [expedition] "Not now" on "Which season?" lasts the session on every GM device; weather then offers a table pick — Neither nags nor resets arbitrarily

- 2026-10-30 — [words] "Checked this turn" in turn modes, "Checked today" in day modes — Words match the mode's step

- 2026-10-30 — [packs] The converter names its output files after the source (<source-slug>-<version>.pack.json, candidates <source-slug>-candidate-<version>), asking for the source name at intake — Files show what they contain at a glance

- 2026-10-30 — [process] Design and build both move into Claude Code: a designer agent (Fable) writes rulings and plans into the repo; a builder agent (Opus) builds from plan files; Sonnet/Haiku for tests, docs sync, pack scripts and searches; an Opus read-only reviewer on each PR — Removes the copy-paste handoff and keeps Fable for design and the hardest build steps
- 2026-10-30 — [process] Budget rules: Fable under 35% of weekly usage, design first, build second, never orchestration; fresh sessions per topic; subagents return summaries — Max 5x caps Fable at 50% of the week and it spends limits about 2.5× faster than Opus
- 2026-10-30 — [process] Mockups as HTML files in docs/design/mockups/; Sunday check-in by calendar reminder; the claude.ai Project archived a month then deleted; PC-only builds for R1; the reviewer comments only until the M5b gate — Rulings on the setup plan's open questions

## 2026-10-25

- 2026-10-25 — [packs] First conversion pack approved: setting-2 kindreds keep no class/level limits under the setting-1 pack's rules, and fairy casting types stay separate — Lets a second setting's characters join a setting-1 campaign without inventing limits.

- 2026-10-25 — [ui] ✓ marks a chosen row only; the row being viewed in list and detail has the heavier border without ✓ — A ✓ on a not-allowed class read as picked.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] Rows reserve a fixed ✓ slot so end-edge values align — The ✓ pushed room-left text out of line.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] Choice cards move on with one tap, no selected state — Fewer taps; nothing to confirm.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] ▲ replaces "!" for not-allowed and broken-rule marks; not-allowed rows never greyed — One shape per meaning; greyed reads as disabled.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] Selected option rows carry a ✓ as well as the heavier border — Never rely on border weight alone.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] Flagged review sections keep their title first, with the flag on the next line — Titles are the scan anchors and wrapped badly on phone.
- 2026-10-25 — [core] The GM's own commands queue offline as intents and replay on reconnect; players see that time is paused — A dropped Wi-Fi connection on the GM's device no longer stops the table.
- 2026-10-25 — [process] Roadmap re-forecast at 30 h/week including M5b, M8b, the design system work and table test 1; R1 complete Jan 24, 2027 — The scope grew by choice; dates reflect it and will be pulled in if pace stays high.

- 2026-10-25 — [ui] On phone the primary action lives in a sticky bottom bar; tablet keeps it top right — Thumb-reachable and always visible.
- 2026-10-25 — [core] Undo over confirm: reversible actions happen at once with an Undo toast; dialogs only for irreversible or high-cost actions — Fewer interruptions, the same safety.
- 2026-10-25 — [words] Words and voice guide adopted, with US English for app text and an automated check — Consistent, plain wording that never hints at hidden things.
- 2026-10-25 — [testing] Ten key journeys with tap, screen and context budgets, tested automatically — The prime directive made measurable.
- 2026-10-25 — [core] Offline states designed: a tappable offline pill with waiting changes, age labels only on stale shared values, rejected changes explained and kept in the log — Nobody is confused or loses work when Wi-Fi drops.
- 2026-10-25 — [ui] Following the conventions: the B25 equip alert becomes a toast with Undo; B17's Choose stays enabled; End expedition keeps its confirm (high-cost); the HP sheet gets a number pad and quick chips that apply at once with Undo — Undo over confirm, never disabled.
